## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: k1s.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero amount, used where a box is empty or an amount does not apply"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: k1s.h_cent
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One cent, a floor that keeps a divisor positive"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: sc.one_cent}

  - id: k1s.h_b1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1. Ordinary business income (loss), or zero if box 1 is empty"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box1_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box1_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2. Net rental real estate income (loss), or zero if box 2 is empty"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box2_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box2_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3. Other net rental income (loss), or zero if box 3 is empty"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box3_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box3
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box3_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 7. Net short-term capital gain (loss), or zero if box 7 is empty"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box7_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box7
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box7_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b8a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8a. Net long-term capital gain (loss), or zero if boxes 8a to 8c are empty"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box8a
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_b8b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8b. Collectibles (28%) gain (loss), or zero if boxes 8a to 8c are empty"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box8b
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.box4_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 4. Interest income (zero if box 4 is empty)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box4_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box4
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box4_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.box5a_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 5a. Ordinary dividends (zero if box 5a is empty)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box5_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box5a
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box5_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.box16a_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 16, code A. Tax-exempt interest income (zero if not shown)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16a_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box16a
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16a_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.box16b_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 16, code B. Other tax-exempt income (zero if not shown)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16b_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box16b
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16b_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.box16c_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 16, code C. Nondeductible expenses (zero if not shown)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16c_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box16c
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16c_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.box16d_if_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 16, code D. Distributions (zero if not shown)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16d_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box16d
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16d_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.gain1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 income (the amount in box 1 if it is income; otherwise zero)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b1, take: {const: 0}}

  - id: k1s.loss1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 loss, as a positive amount (zero if box 1 is not a loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_zero, take: k1s.h_b1}

  - id: k1s.gain2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 income (the amount in box 2 if it is income; otherwise zero)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b2, take: {const: 0}}

  - id: k1s.loss2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 loss, as a positive amount (zero if box 2 is not a loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_zero, take: k1s.h_b2}

  - id: k1s.gain3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 income (the amount in box 3 if it is income; otherwise zero)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b3, take: {const: 0}}

  - id: k1s.loss3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 loss, as a positive amount (zero if box 3 is not a loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_zero, take: k1s.h_b3}

  - id: k1s.gain7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 7 net short-term capital gain (zero if box 7 is not a gain)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b7, take: {const: 0}}

  - id: k1s.loss7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 7 net short-term capital loss, as a positive amount (zero if not a loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_zero, take: k1s.h_b7}

  - id: k1s.gain8
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8a net long-term capital gain (zero if box 8a is not a gain)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b8a, take: {const: 0}}

  - id: k1s.loss8
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8a net long-term capital loss, as a positive amount (zero if not a loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_zero, take: k1s.h_b8a}

  - id: k1s.h_gain8b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8b collectibles (28%) gain (zero if not a gain)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_b8b, take: {const: 0}}

  - id: k1s.h_loss8b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8b collectibles (28%) loss, as a positive amount (zero if not a loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: k1s.h_zero, take: k1s.h_b8b}

  - id: k1s.h_has_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Is there a loss in box 1, 2, 3, 7 or 8a?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.loss1, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.loss2, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.loss3, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.loss7, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.loss8, {const: 0}]}

  - id: k1s.h_risk_gate
    printed: "(gate)"
    label: "At-risk limitations: a loss from an activity with amounts for which you aren't at risk (Form 6198)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.not_at_risk, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_has_loss, {const: true}]}
    note: "Generally, with a loss and amounts not at risk you will have to complete Form 6198; the tool stops instead of computing the at-risk limit."

  - id: k1s.f7203_required
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203 is filed by shareholders who claim a deduction for their share of an aggregate loss, receive a non-dividend distribution, dispose of stock, or receive a loan repayment"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.loss1, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.loss2, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.loss3, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.loss7, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.loss8, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, gt: [k1s.box16d_if_any, {const: 0}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box16ef, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry]
    note: "Open: the interface k1s.box16ef combines code E (loan repayment, which requires Form 7203) with code F (foreign taxes, reported on Form 7203 line 46). Chose to treat either as requiring Form 7203. Disposition of stock and a loss carried over for basis are refused rather than computed."

  - id: k1s.h_c35
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203, line 35, column (c): ordinary business loss allowed from stock basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.c35}

  - id: k1s.h_c36
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203, line 36, column (c): net rental real estate loss allowed from stock basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.c36}

  - id: k1s.h_c37
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203, line 37, column (c): other net rental loss allowed from stock basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.c37}

  - id: k1s.h_c38
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 7203, line 38, column (c): net capital loss allowed from stock basis"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f7203, field: f7203.c38}

  - id: k1s.h_allow1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 loss after applying the basis limitation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [k1s.loss1, k1s.h_c35]}
    refuses: [k1s.h_risk_gate, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.basis_carry]

  - id: k1s.h_allow2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 loss after applying the basis limitation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [k1s.loss2, k1s.h_c36]}
    refuses: [k1s.h_risk_gate, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.basis_carry]

  - id: k1s.h_allow3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 3 loss after applying the basis limitation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [k1s.loss3, k1s.h_c37]}
    refuses: [k1s.h_risk_gate, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.basis_carry]

  - id: k1s.h_caploss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Capital losses reported in boxes 7 and 8a"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.loss7, k1s.loss8]}

  - id: k1s.h_caploss_w
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Capital losses in boxes 7 and 8a, not less than one cent (divisor)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [k1s.h_caploss, k1s.h_cent]}

  - id: k1s.h_allow7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 7 loss after applying the basis limitation (pro rata share of the allowed net capital loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: prorate, amount: k1s.loss7, part: k1s.h_c38, whole: k1s.h_caploss_w, places: 6}
    refuses: [k1s.h_risk_gate, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.basis_carry]
    note: "Open: Form 7203 line 38 holds one net capital loss; when the basis limit applies, the losses allowed are allocated on a pro rata basis (Regs. 1.1366-2(a)(4) and (5)). Chose to allocate line 38 column (c) between the box 7 and box 8a losses in proportion to those losses, the ratio rounded to 6 places. Where no limit applies the ratio is 1 and the full loss is allowed."

  - id: k1s.h_allow8
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8a loss after applying the basis limitation (pro rata share of the allowed net capital loss)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: prorate, amount: k1s.loss8, part: k1s.h_c38, whole: k1s.h_caploss_w, places: 6}
    refuses: [k1s.h_risk_gate, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.basis_carry]

  - id: k1s.h_allow8b
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8b loss after applying the basis limitation (same proportion as the capital losses)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: prorate, amount: k1s.h_loss8b, part: k1s.h_c38, whole: k1s.h_caploss_w, places: 6}
    refuses: [k1s.h_risk_gate, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.basis_carry]
    note: "Open: box 8b is part of the long-term amount in box 8a. Chose to apply to it the same allowed fraction as to the capital losses."

  - id: k1s.st
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 7. After applying the limitations on losses and deductions, report the net short-term capital gain (loss) on Schedule D (Form 1040), line 5"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: k1s.gain7, take: k1s.h_allow7}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box6, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.h_risk_gate]

  - id: k1s.lt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8a. After applying the limitations on losses and deductions, report the net long-term capital gain (loss) on Schedule D (Form 1040), line 12"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: k1s.gain8, take: k1s.h_allow8}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box6, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.h_risk_gate]

  - id: k1s.g28
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8b. After applying the limitations on losses and deductions, report collectibles gain or loss on line 4 of the 28% Rate Gain Worksheet—Line 18"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: k1s.h_gain8b, take: k1s.h_allow8b}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box6, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.h_risk_gate]

  - id: k1s.u1250
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 8c. Unrecaptured section 1250 gain from the sale or exchange of the corporation's business assets (Unrecaptured Section 1250 Gain Worksheet, line 5)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box8c
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box8_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.box8_stmt]
    note: "If the corporation reports the other two types of unrecaptured gain (worksheet lines 10 and 11), it provides an attached statement; that case is refused."

  - id: k1s.int
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 4. Report interest income on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 2b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: k1s.box4_if_any}

  - id: k1s.ord_div
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 5a. Report ordinary dividends on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 3b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: k1s.box5a_if_any}

  - id: k1s.qual_div
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 5b. Report any qualified dividends on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 3a"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box5_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box5b
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box5_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.backup
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 13, code O. Backup withholding: include this amount in the total you enter on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 25c"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box13o_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.box13o
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box13o_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.taxexempt
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 16, code A. Individual shareholders include tax-exempt interest on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 2a"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: k1s.box16a_if_any}

  - id: k1s.h_mp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Material participation: you materially participated in the corporation's trade or business activity (any of the seven tests)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.work, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.hours500, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.most_hours, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.past_years, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [k1s.mp_other, {const: true}]}

  - id: k1s.h_p4
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 activity goes in Form 8582 Part IV: you actively participated (at least 10% interest), and not married filing separately"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.active, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.tenth, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
    note: "Open: married individuals filing separately who lived with their spouses at any time during the year must use Part V; the interface has no question on living apart, so every married-filing-separately return is routed to Part V. The box 2 amount is always treated as passive: there is no interface for the real estate professional exception."

  - id: k1s.h_pr1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss of the box 1 activity (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.b1_prior
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_pr2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss of the box 2 activity (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.b2_prior
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_pr3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss of the box 3 activity (2024 Form 8582, Part VII, column (c))"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.b3_prior
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.prior_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.p4_income
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part IV, column (a): current year net income of the rental real estate activity with active participation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.gain2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box6, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12]

  - id: k1s.p4_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part IV, column (b): current year net loss of the rental real estate activity with active participation (after the basis and at-risk limitations)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_allow2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box6, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.h_risk_gate]

  - id: k1s.p4_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part IV, column (c): prior-year unallowed losses of the rental real estate activity with active participation"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_pr2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry]
    note: "Open: a prior-year loss belongs in Part IV only if you actively participated both in the year it arose and in 2025; the interface does not ask about the earlier year, so the prior loss follows the 2025 classification."

  - id: k1s.h_p5_inc1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 income from a trade or business activity in which you didn't materially participate"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_mp, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.gain1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_mp, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_p5_inc2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 income from a passive rental real estate activity that doesn't qualify for the special allowance"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.gain2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.p5_income
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part V, column (a): current year net income of passive trade or business and other rental activities"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_p5_inc1, k1s.h_p5_inc2, k1s.gain3]}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box6, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12]

  - id: k1s.h_p5_loss1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 1 loss (after basis and at-risk limits) from a trade or business activity in which you didn't materially participate"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_mp, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_allow1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_mp, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.h_p5_loss2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 2 loss (after basis and at-risk limits) from a passive rental real estate activity that doesn't qualify for the special allowance"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_allow2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.p5_loss
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part V, column (b): current year net loss of passive trade or business and other rental activities"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_p5_loss1, k1s.h_p5_loss2, k1s.h_allow3]}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box6, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.h_risk_gate]

  - id: k1s.h_p5_pr2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Prior-year unallowed loss of the box 2 activity when it is not in Part IV"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_pr2
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_p4, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero

  - id: k1s.p5_prior
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8582 Part V, column (c): unallowed losses for the prior years for each activity"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.h_pr1, k1s.h_p5_pr2, k1s.h_pr3]}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry]
    note: "Open: the text does not say how a prior-year unallowed loss of the box 1 activity is reported once you materially participate in it. Chose to keep it in Part V whether or not you materially participate in 2025."

  - id: k1s.nonpassive
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Nonpassive income (loss) from this corporation on Schedule E, line 28: column (k) less column (i)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: k1s.col_k, take: k1s.col_i}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box6, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.h_risk_gate]

  - id: k1s.h_vother_gate
    printed: "(gate)"
    label: "The code V statement shows section 199A dividends or qualified PTP income"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box17v_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.v_other, {const: true}]}

  - id: k1s.qbi
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Box 17, code V. Section 199A information: qualified business income (loss) from the S corporation"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box17v_any, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.qbi_stmt
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.box17v_any, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.h_vother_gate, k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry]
    note: "Open: you must include only items allowed in determining taxable income for the year; a loss suspended by the basis or passive activity limits would reduce the statement's QBI loss. The interface carries only the statement amount; this limitation is recorded here rather than computed."

  - id: k1s.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One Schedule K-1 (count)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: person.one}

  - id: k1s.sche_any
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "This Schedule K-1 has an amount in box 1, 2 or 3 to report on Schedule E (Form 1040), line 28"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box1_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box2_any, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [k1s.box3_any, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box6, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.box13_other, k1s.box14, k1s.box15]

  - id: k1s.h_show_k
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Column (k) of Schedule E, line 28 has an amount"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [k1s.col_k, {const: 0}]}

  - id: k1s.h_show_i
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Column (i) of Schedule E, line 28 has an amount"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [k1s.col_i, {const: 0}]}

  - id: k1s.h_show_h
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Column (h) of Schedule E, line 28 has an amount"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [k1s.col_h, {const: 0}]}

  - id: k1s.col_k
    printed: "28 (k)"
    label: "Report box 1 income (loss) from corporate trade or business activities in which you materially participated in column (i) or (k) of Schedule E (Form 1040), line 28 — nonpassive income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_mp, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.gain1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_mp, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box6, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12]
    shown_when: k1s.h_show_k
    note: "Box 2 income is treated as passive (no interface for the real estate professional exception); gambling and other box 10 items are refused."

  - id: k1s.col_i
    printed: "28 (i)"
    label: "Report box 1 income (loss) from corporate trade or business activities in which you materially participated in column (i) or (k) of Schedule E (Form 1040), line 28 — nonpassive loss, after applying the basis and at-risk limitations"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_mp, {const: true}]}
          then: k1s.h_allow1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [k1s.h_mp, {const: false}]}
          then: k1s.h_zero
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box6, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12, k1s.h_risk_gate]
    shown_when: k1s.h_show_i
    note: "The basis limit is taken from Form 7203, line 35, column (c) (stock basis); debt basis (column (d)) is not among the supplied interfaces. The excess business loss limitation is applied on Form 461, not here."

  - id: k1s.col_h
    printed: "28 (h)"
    label: "Passive income: report the income in column (h) of Schedule E (Form 1040), line 28 (box 1 income from activities in which you didn't materially participate, and box 2 and box 3 income)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [k1s.p4_income, k1s.p5_income]}
    refuses: [k1s.multi, k1s.disposed, k1s.basis_carry, k1s.risk_carry, k1s.box6, k1s.box9, k1s.box10, k1s.box11_12]
    shown_when: k1s.h_show_h
```
